{"forms":[{"form":"anie","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"anny","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"ony","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"glosses":["To even the slightest extent; at all."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"If you get any taller, you'll start having to duck through doorways!","type":"example"},{"text":"Is she any less enthused now that it's raining?","type":"example"},{"text":"Is her enthusiasm any less now that it's raining?","type":"example"},{"text":"You don't seem to be any the worse after such a tragic experience.","type":"example"},{"text":"I will not remain here any longer.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To even the slightest extent; at all.","Used before different, before comparatives with more and less, and idiomatically before other comparatives, generally in interrogative, hypothetical, or negative contexts."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"That doesn't bother me any.","type":"example"},{"text":"Unfortunately, I don't expect the documentation to help any.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To even the slightest extent; at all.","Used to modify a verb, generally a finite verb or a non-finite verb in object position."],"tags":["US","not-comparable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɛni/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/æni/"},{"ipa":"/ɪni/","tags":["US","pin-pen-merger"]}],"upstream_line":17279,"upstream_offset":118238731,"upstream_record_sha256":"c57e90aece20ab342ae1c864cdef0a434c7b59c3c68a00d826d1a13708bd49c8","word":"any"}
{"forms":[{"form":"anie","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"anny","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"ony","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"det","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"zero"},{"word":"no"}],"examples":[{"text":"Do you have any biscuits?","type":"example"},{"text":"Do you have any food?","type":"example"},{"text":"Do you think there's any point to/in trying to explain?","type":"example"},{"text":"It won't do you any good.","type":"example"},{"text":"\"Give me your pen.\" — \"I don't have any pen.\"","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One at all; at least one; at least one kind of; some; a positive quantity of."],"raw_tags":["in the negative or interrogative","with plural or uncountable nouns"],"synonyms":[{"word":"some"},{"word":"all"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"Choose any item you want.","type":"example"},{"text":"Any person may apply.","type":"example"},{"text":"Representative democracy is better than any other system.","type":"example"},{"text":"Press any key to continue.","type":"example"},{"text":"The character '#' matches any digit 0–9.","type":"example"},{"text":"Please bring some plates — any plates will do.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'll drink any whiskey you've got.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A/an, each or some, no matter its/their identity or nature."]},{"examples":[{"text":"They'll be arriving any day.","type":"example"},{"text":"I expect the phone to ring at any moment.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An unspecified but imminent (second, minute, day etc.)."],"raw_tags":["with time designations"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɛni/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/æni/"},{"ipa":"/ɪni/","tags":["US","pin-pen-merger"]}],"upstream_line":17280,"upstream_offset":118258394,"upstream_record_sha256":"f40f9242cd2fe3f15c5ea712a52ea5a285d54572315dd20ba33b392f96437ed4","word":"any"}
